| 12510 | verified ("suggested answer") | Moonfall: Flurry of Blades kills all my units at the chosen battlefield in response — still resolves? | (RJ: yes, -2 still applies; "where you have units" only restricts the choice, citing 355.10.b) | MISMATCH-RJ → DECIDED (#12510): confirmed | RJ#12510 … asserts no -2. CR 355.10.b only says my units aren't targets; the battlefield is the target and "where you have units" is its targeting restriction (355.9.b), rechecked on resolution: "A target is illegal on resolution if it no longer meets the spell's targeting requirements" (359.3.e.2), so instructions about it ("there") are ignored (359.3.e.5/e.12). Same as DECISIONS principle 21. (CR text read at app.riftjudge.com/rules/core) Related 10053, 11046, 2006 |
